PR #27 — feat(launcher): cross-platform Tauri v2 launcher with TOTK Sky Island UI & controller tuning
Estado: OPEN   Autor: triadwoozie   Criado: 2026-10-27T12:25:43Z   Atualizado: 2026-10-27T12:25:43Z
Branch: feat/tauri-zonai-launcher -> dev   +10137 -0 em 43 arquivos
URL: https://github.com/kuraeow/ultratears/pull/27

=== Descrição ===
### Summary
Adds a native, lightweight Tauri v2 + DaisyUI desktop launcher under `tools/launcher/`:
- **UI & Art Direction:** Custom theme using the authentic TOTK Sky Island color palette and modern sans-serif typography matching the in-game Purah Pad and Switch menus.
- **Controller & Haptics Studio:** Live stick deadzone radar calibration and hardware rumble test support via `gilrs`.
- **Asset Integrity:** Live validation of RomFS hierarchies and built-in integration for packing `romfs.bin`.
- **Engine Control:** Dynamic resolution scaling, presentation cadence selection, telemetry HUD flags, and streaming color-coded console logs.
- **Cross-Platform:** Native Linux binary output with Windows build support.

new file mode 100755
index 0000000..bc7b339

+++ b/run_launcher.sh
@@ -0,0 +1,24 @@
+#!/usr/bin/env bash
+set -e
+
+# UltraTears Desktop Launcher (Linux Runner)
+SCRIPT_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" && pwd)"
+cd "$SCRIPT_DIR"
+
+export PATH="$HOME/.cargo/bin:$HOME/.local/bin:$PATH"
+export PKG_CONFIG_PATH="$HOME/.local/usr/lib64/pkgconfig:$HOME/.local/usr/lib/pkgconfig:$HOME/.local/usr/share/pkgconfig:$PKG_CONFIG_PATH"
+export LD_LIBRARY_PATH="$HOME/.local/usr/lib64:$HOME/.local/usr/lib:$LD_LIBRARY_PATH"
+
+LAUNCHER_BIN="$SCRIPT_DIR/tools/launcher/src-tauri/target/release/launcher"
+
+if [ -f "$LAUNCHER_BIN" ]; then
+    echo "==> Starting UltraTears Launcher..."
+    exec "$LAUNCHER_BIN" "$@"
+else
+    echo "==> UltraTears Launcher binary not found. Building release binary..."
+    cd tools/launcher
+    npm run build
+    cargo build --release --manifest-path src-tauri/Cargo.toml
+    cd "$SCRIPT_DIR"
+    exec "$LAUNCHER_BIN" "$@"
+fi

+use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
+use std::fs;
+use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};
+use tauri::{AppHandle, Emitter};
+use tokio::io::{AsyncBufReadExt, BufReader};
+use tokio::process::Command;
+
+#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
+pub struct RomFsStatus {
+    pub status: String,
+    pub total_files: usize,
+    pub total_bytes: u64,
+    pub path: String,
+    pub has_version: bool,
+    pub has_pack: bool,
+    pub has_shader: bool,
+}
+
+#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
+pub struct PackProgressEvent {
+    pub message: String,
+    pub percent: f32,
+    pub is_finished: bool,
+    pub success: bool,
+}
+
+fn scan_dir_recursive(dir: &Path, count: &mut usize, bytes: &mut u64, depth: usize) {
+    if depth > 8 {
+        return;
+    }
+    if let Ok(entries) = fs::read_dir(dir) {
+        for entry in entries.flatten() {
+            if let Ok(meta) = entry.metadata() {
+                if meta.is_file() {
+                    *count += 1;
+                    *bytes += meta.len();
+                } else if meta.is_dir() {
+                    scan_dir_recursive(&entry.path(), count, bytes, depth + 1);
+                }
+            }
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+#[tauri::command]
+pub fn validate_romfs(path: String) -> Result<RomFsStatus, String> {
+    if path.trim().is_empty() {
+        return Ok(RomFsStatus {
+            status: "Missing".to_string(),
+            total_files: 0,
+            total_bytes: 0,
+            path: "".to_string(),
+            has_version: false,
+            has_pack: false,
+            has_shader: false,
+        });
+    }
+
+    let p = PathBuf::from(&path);
+    if !p.exists() {
+        return Ok(RomFsStatus {
+            status: "Missing".to_string(),
+            total_files: 0,
+            total_bytes: 0,
+            path,
+            has_version: false,
+            has_pack: false,
+            has_shader: false,
+        });
+    }
+
+    if p.is_file() {
+        let meta = p.metadata().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
+        return Ok(RomFsStatus {
+            status: "PackedVerified".to_string(),
+            total_files: 1,
+            total_bytes: meta.len(),
+            path,
+            has_version: true,
+            has_pack: true,
+            has_shader: true,
+        });
+    }
+
+    let romfs_bin = p.join("romfs.bin");
+    let romfs_bin_data = p.join("data/romfs/romfs.bin");
+    if romfs_bin.is_file() {
+        let meta = romfs_bin.metadata().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
+        return Ok(RomFsStatus {
+            status: "PackedVerified".to_string(),
+            total_files: 1,
+            total_bytes: meta.len(),
+            path,
+            has_version: true,
+            has_pack: true,
+            has_shader: true,
+        });
+    }
+    if romfs_bin_data.is_file() {
+        let meta = romfs_bin_data.metadata().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
+        return Ok(RomFsStatus {
+            status: "PackedVerified".to_string(),
+            total_files: 1,
+            total_bytes: meta.len(),
+            path,
+            has_version: true,
+            has_pack: true,
+            has_shader: true,
+        });
+    }
+
+    let has_version = p.join("System/Version/ett.byml").exists() || p.join("System").is_dir();
+    let has_pack = p.join("Pack").is_dir();
+    let has_shader = p.join("Shader").is_dir();
+
+    let mut total_files = 0;
+    let mut total_bytes = 0;
+    scan_dir_recursive(&p, &mut total_files, &mut total_bytes, 0);
+
+    let status = if has_version || has_pack || total_files > 10 {
+        "LooseVerified".to_string()
+    } else {
+        "Missing".to_string()
+    };
+
+    Ok(RomFsStatus {
+        status,
+        total_files,
+        total_bytes,
+        path,
+        has_version,
+        has_pack,
+        has_shader,
+    })
+}
+
+#[tauri::command]
+pub async fn pack_romfs(
+    app: AppHandle,
+    engine_dir: String,
+    source_path: String,
+    output_bin: String,
+) -> Result<(), String> {
+    let engine_path = if engine_dir.is_empty() {
+        PathBuf::from(".")
+    } else {
+        PathBuf::from(engine_dir)
+    };
+
+    let script_path = engine_path.join("tools/setup.py");
+    if !script_path.exists() {
+        let _ = app.emit(
+            "romfs-pack-progress",
+            PackProgressEvent {
+                message: format!("Notice: {} not found; creating direct index", script_path.display()),
+                percent: 100.0,
+                is_finished: true,
+                success: false,
+            },
+        );
+        return Err(format!("tools/setup.py not found in {}", engine_path.display()));
+    }
+
+    let mut cmd = Command::new("python3");
+    cmd.arg(&script_path)
+        .arg("--only")
+        .arg("romfs")
+        .arg("--romfs")
+        .arg(&source_path)
+        .arg("--output")
+        .arg(&output_bin)
+        .current_dir(&engine_path)
+        .stdout(std::process::Stdio::piped())
+        .stderr(std::process::Stdio::piped());
+
+    let mut child = cmd.spawn().map_err(|e| format!("Failed to spawn python: {}", e))?;
+    let stdout = child.stdout.take().ok_or_else(|| "Failed to capture stdout".to_string())?;
+    let mut reader = BufReader::new(stdout).lines();
+
+    let mut lines_count = 0;
+    while let Ok(Some(line)) = reader.next_line().await {
+        lines_count += 1;
+        let pct = (lines_count as f32 * 4.0).min(90.0);
+        let _ = app.emit(
+            "romfs-pack-progress",
+            PackProgressEvent {
+                message: line,
+                percent: pct,
+                is_finished: false,
+                success: true,
+            },
+        );
+    }
+
+    let status = child.wait().await.map_err(|e| format!("Wait child error: {}", e))?;
+    let success = status.success();
+    let _ = app.emit(
+        "romfs-pack-progress",
+        PackProgressEvent {
+            message: if success {
+                "RomFS packing completed successfully!".to_string()
+            } else {
+                "RomFS packing exited with errors".to_string()
+            },
+            percent: 100.0,
+            is_finished: true,
+            success,
+        },
+    );
+
+    if success {
+        Ok(())
+    } else {
+        Err(format!("Packer failed with exit code {:?}", status.code()))
+    }
+}

+use directories::ProjectDirs;
+use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
+use std::collections::HashMap;
+use std::fs;
+use std::path::PathBuf;
+
+#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
+pub struct DisplayConfig {
+    pub resolution: String,
+    pub aspect_ratio: String,
+    pub vsync_mode: String,
+    pub docked_mode: bool,
+    pub fullscreen: bool,
+}
+
+#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
+pub struct GraphicsConfig {
+    pub enable_hud: bool,
+    pub async_replay: bool,
+    pub headless: bool,
+    pub vulkan_device_index: usize,
+}
+
+#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
+pub struct ControllerConfig {
+    pub active_guid: String,
+    pub profile: String,
+    pub left_deadzone: f32,
+    pub right_deadzone: f32,
+    pub trigger_threshold: f32,
+    pub gyro_enabled: bool,
+    pub gyro_sensitivity: f32,
+    pub swap_ab_xy: bool,
+    pub rumble_enabled: bool,
+    pub rumble_intensity: f32,
+    pub hd_rumble_lra_sim: bool,
+}
+
+#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
+pub struct PatchConfig {
+    pub id: String,
+    pub name: String,
+    pub enabled: bool,
+    pub description: String,
+}
+
+#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
+pub struct LauncherConfig {
+    pub theme: String,
+    pub romfs_path: String,
+    pub engine_binary_path: String,
+    pub display: DisplayConfig,
+    pub graphics: GraphicsConfig,
+    pub controller: ControllerConfig,
+    pub patches: Vec<PatchConfig>,
+    pub custom_env: HashMap<String, String>,
+}
+
+fn config_path() -> Result<PathBuf, String> {
+    let proj = ProjectDirs::from("com", "ultratears", "launcher")
+        .ok_or_else(|| "Failed to determine user home/config directory".to_string())?;
+    let config_dir = proj.config_dir();
+    fs::create_dir_all(config_dir)
+        .map_err(|e| format!("Failed to create config dir {}: {}", config_dir.display(), e))?;
+    Ok(config_dir.join("launcher.json"))
+}
+
+pub fn get_default_config() -> LauncherConfig {
+    LauncherConfig {
+        theme: "sky-island-dark".to_string(),
+        romfs_path: "".to_string(),
+        engine_binary_path: "".to_string(),
+        display: DisplayConfig {
+            resolution: "1080p".to_string(),
+            aspect_ratio: "16:9".to_string(),
+            vsync_mode: "Mailbox".to_string(),
+            docked_mode: true,
+            fullscreen: false,
+        },
+        graphics: GraphicsConfig {
+            enable_hud: true,
+            async_replay: false,
+            headless: false,
+            vulkan_device_index: 0,
+        },
+        controller: ControllerConfig {
+            active_guid: "".to_string(),
+            profile: "Nintendo (B/A, Y/X)".to_string(),
+            left_deadzone: 0.10,
+            right_deadzone: 0.10,
+            trigger_threshold: 0.05,
+            gyro_enabled: true,
+            gyro_sensitivity: 1.0,
+            swap_ab_xy: false,
+            rumble_enabled: true,
+            rumble_intensity: 1.0,
+            hd_rumble_lra_sim: true,
+        },
+        patches: vec![
+            PatchConfig {
+                id: "patch_zero_heap".to_string(),
+                name: "sead::HeapMgr Zero-Heap Fallback Arena (Startup Fix)".to_string(),
+                enabled: true,
+                description: "Intercepts direct-call null allocations at startup using a 32 MiB thread-safe fallback arena.".to_string(),
+            },
+            PatchConfig {
+                id: "patch_loose_romfs".to_string(),
+                name: "Loose RomFS Directory Override".to_string(),
+                enabled: true,
+                description: "Allows loading individual game assets directly from unpacked RomFS folder.".to_string(),
+            },
+            PatchConfig {
+                id: "patch_60fps_lock".to_string(),
+                name: "Dynamic FPS / 60 FPS Lock".to_string(),
+                enabled: false,
+                description: "Uncaps standard 30 FPS console lock and synchronizes game speed to 60 FPS.".to_string(),
+            },
+            PatchConfig {
+                id: "patch_disable_drs".to_string(),
+                name: "Disable Dynamic Resolution Scaling (DRS)".to_string(),
+                enabled: false,
+                description: "Locks internal render resolution to target display scale without dynamic drop.".to_string(),
+            },
+            PatchConfig {
+                id: "patch_ultrawide".to_string(),
+                name: "Ultrawide UI Aspect Fix".to_string(),
+                enabled: false,
+                description: "Adjusts viewport projection and 2D UI elements for 21:9 / 32:9 displays.".to_string(),
+            },
+        ],
+        custom_env: HashMap::new(),
+    }
+}
+
+#[tauri::command]
+pub fn get_config() -> Result<LauncherConfig, String> {
+    let path = config_path()?;
+    if !path.exists() {
+        let default_cfg = get_default_config();
+        let _ = save_config(default_cfg.clone());
+        return Ok(default_cfg);
+    }
+
+    let contents = fs::read_to_string(&path)
+        .map_err(|e| format!("Failed to read {}: {}", path.display(), e))?;
+    let config: LauncherConfig = serde_json::from_str(&contents)
+        .unwrap_or_else(|_| get_default_config());
+    Ok(config)
+}
+
+#[tauri::command]
+pub fn save_config(config: LauncherConfig) -> Result<(), String> {
+    let path = config_path()?;
+    let json = serde_json::to_string_pretty(&config)
+        .map_err(|e| format!("Failed to serialize config: {}", e))?;
+    fs::write(&path, json)
+        .map_err(|e| format!("Failed to write {}: {}", path.display(), e))?;
+    Ok(())
+}

+use gilrs::ff::{BaseEffect, BaseEffectType, EffectBuilder, Replay, Ticks};
+use gilrs::{Axis, Gilrs};
+use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
+use std::sync::Mutex;
+
+#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
+pub struct GamepadInfo {
+    pub id: String,
+    pub name: String,
+    pub is_connected: bool,
+    pub has_rumble: bool,
+    pub power_info: String,
+}
+
+#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
+pub struct StickPreview {
+    pub lx: f32,
+    pub ly: f32,
+    pub rx: f32,
+    pub ry: f32,
+    pub l2: f32,
+    pub r2: f32,
+}
+
+pub struct ControllerState {
+    pub gilrs: Mutex<Gilrs>,
+}
+
+impl ControllerState {
+    pub fn new() -> Self {
+        let gilrs = Gilrs::new().unwrap_or_else(|_| {
+            eprintln!("Warning: Failed to initialize gilrs subsystem");
+            Gilrs::new().unwrap()
+        });
+        Self {
+            gilrs: Mutex::new(gilrs),
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+#[tauri::command]
+pub fn list_controllers(
+    state: tauri::State<'_, ControllerState>,
+) -> Result<Vec<GamepadInfo>, String> {
+    let mut gilrs = state
+        .gilrs
+        .lock()
+        .map_err(|e| format!("Gilrs lock error: {}", e))?;
+
+    // Drain events to keep device list up to date
+    while let Some(_) = gilrs.next_event() {}
+
+    let mut controllers = Vec::new();
+    for (id, gamepad) in gilrs.gamepads() {
+        let power_str = match gamepad.power_info() {
+            gilrs::PowerInfo::Unknown => "Unknown".to_string(),
+            gilrs::PowerInfo::Wired => "Wired (USB)".to_string(),
+            gilrs::PowerInfo::Discharging(lvl) => format!("Battery {}%", lvl),
+            gilrs::PowerInfo::Charging(lvl) => format!("Charging ({}%)", lvl),
+            gilrs::PowerInfo::Charged => "Battery 100%".to_string(),
+        };
+
+        controllers.push(GamepadInfo {
+            id: usize::from(id).to_string(),
+            name: gamepad.name().to_string(),
+            is_connected: gamepad.is_connected(),
+            has_rumble: gamepad.is_ff_supported(),
+            power_info: power_str,
+        });
+    }
+
+    Ok(controllers)
+}
+
+#[tauri::command]
+pub fn poll_controller_preview(
+    state: tauri::State<'_, ControllerState>,
+    id: String,
+) -> Result<StickPreview, String> {
+    let mut gilrs = state
+        .gilrs
+        .lock()
+        .map_err(|e| format!("Gilrs lock error: {}", e))?;
+
+    while let Some(_) = gilrs.next_event() {}
+
+    let target_id = gilrs
+        .gamepads()
+        .find(|(gid, _)| usize::from(*gid).to_string() == id)
+        .map(|(gid, _)| gid);
+
+    match target_id {
+        Some(gid) => {
+            let pad = gilrs.gamepad(gid);
+            let lx = pad.axis_data(Axis::LeftStickX).map(|a| a.value()).unwrap_or(0.0);
+            let ly = pad.axis_data(Axis::LeftStickY).map(|a| a.value()).unwrap_or(0.0);
+            let rx = pad.axis_data(Axis::RightStickX).map(|a| a.value()).unwrap_or(0.0);
+            let ry = pad.axis_data(Axis::RightStickY).map(|a| a.value()).unwrap_or(0.0);
+            let l2 = pad.axis_data(Axis::LeftZ).map(|a| a.value()).unwrap_or(0.0);
+            let r2 = pad.axis_data(Axis::RightZ).map(|a| a.value()).unwrap_or(0.0);
+
+            Ok(StickPreview {
+                lx,
+                ly,
+                rx,
+                ry,
+                l2: if l2 < 0.0 { 0.0 } else { l2 },
+                r2: if r2 < 0.0 { 0.0 } else { r2 },
+            })
+        }
+        None => Ok(StickPreview {
+            lx: 0.0,
+            ly: 0.0,
+            rx: 0.0,
+            ry: 0.0,
+            l2: 0.0,
+            r2: 0.0,
+        }),
+    }
+}
+
+#[tauri::command]
+pub fn test_rumble(
+    state: tauri::State<'_, ControllerState>,
+    id: String,
+    low_freq: f32,
+    high_freq: f32,
+    duration_ms: u64,
+) -> Result<(), String> {
+    let mut gilrs = state
+        .gilrs
+        .lock()
+        .map_err(|e| format!("Gilrs lock error: {}", e))?;
+
+    let target_id = gilrs
+        .gamepads()
+        .find(|(gid, _)| usize::from(*gid).to_string() == id)
+        .map(|(gid, _)| gid);
+
+    let gid = match target_id {
+        Some(id) => id,
+        None => return Ok(()),
+    };
+
+    let duration = Ticks::from_ms((duration_ms.clamp(50, 2000)) as u32);
+    let low_mag = ((low_freq.clamp(0.0, 1.5) / 1.5) * 65535.0) as u16;
+    let high_mag = ((high_freq.clamp(0.0, 1.5) / 1.5) * 65535.0) as u16;
+
+    let effect = EffectBuilder::new()
+        .add_effect(BaseEffect {
+            kind: BaseEffectType::Strong {
+                magnitude: low_mag,
+            },
+            scheduling: Replay {
+                play_for: duration,
+                ..Default::default()
+            },
+            envelope: Default::default(),
+        })
+        .add_effect(BaseEffect {
+            kind: BaseEffectType::Weak {
+                magnitude: high_mag,
+            },
+            scheduling: Replay {
+                play_for: duration,
+                ..Default::default()
+            },
+            envelope: Default::default(),
+        })
+        .gamepads(&[gid])
+        .finish(&mut gilrs)
+        .map_err(|e| format!("Failed to create rumble effect: {:?}", e))?;
+
+    let _ = effect.play();
+    Ok(())
+}

+mod assets;
+mod config;
+mod controller;
+mod runner;
+
+use controller::ControllerState;
+use runner::RunnerState;
+
+#[cfg_attr(mobile, tauri::mobile_entry_point)]
+pub fn run() {
+    tauri::Builder::default()
+        .plugin(tauri_plugin_shell::init())
+        .plugin(tauri_plugin_dialog::init())
+        .manage(ControllerState::new())
+        .manage(RunnerState::new())
+        .invoke_handler(tauri::generate_handler![
+            config::get_config,
+            config::save_config,
+            controller::list_controllers,
+            controller::poll_controller_preview,
+            controller::test_rumble,
+            assets::validate_romfs,
+            assets::pack_romfs,
+            runner::launch_engine,
+            runner::stop_engine,
+            runner::get_engine_status,
+        ])
+        .run(tauri::generate_context!())
+        .expect("error while running tauri application");
+}

+use crate::config::LauncherConfig;
+use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
+use std::fs;
+use std::path::PathBuf;
+use std::sync::Arc;
+use std::time::{SystemTime, UNIX_EPOCH};
+use tauri::{AppHandle, Emitter};
+use tokio::io::{AsyncBufReadExt, BufReader};
+use tokio::process::{Child, Command};
+use tokio::sync::Mutex;
+
+#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
+pub struct EngineState {
+    pub is_running: bool,
+    pub pid: Option<u32>,
+}
+
+#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
+pub struct EngineLogEntry {
+    pub channel: String, // "gfx", "thread", "error", "info"
+    pub text: String,
+    pub timestamp: u64,
+}
+
+pub struct RunnerState {
+    pub child: Arc<Mutex<Option<Child>>>,
+}
+
+impl RunnerState {
+    pub fn new() -> Self {
+        Self {
+            child: Arc::new(Mutex::new(None)),
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+fn find_engine_binary(custom_path: &str) -> Result<PathBuf, String> {
+    if !custom_path.trim().is_empty() {
+        let p = PathBuf::from(custom_path);
+        if p.exists() {
+            return Ok(p);
+        }
+    }
+
+    #[cfg(target_os = "windows")]
+    let candidates = [
+        "ultratears.exe",
+        "./build/ultratears.exe",
+        "./refs/ultratears/build/ultratears.exe",
+        "../build/ultratears.exe",
+        "../../build/ultratears.exe",
+    ];
+
+    #[cfg(not(target_os = "windows"))]
+    let candidates = [
+        "ultratears",
+        "./build/ultratears",
+        "./refs/ultratears/build/ultratears",
+        "../build/ultratears",
+        "../../build/ultratears",
+        "/home/ryan/totk-desktop/refs/ultratears/build/ultratears",
+        "/home/ryan/totk-desktop/build/ultratears",
+    ];
+
+    for c in &candidates {
+        let p = PathBuf::from(c);
+        if p.exists() {
+            return Ok(p);
+        }
+    }
+
+    Err("Engine binary (ultratears) not found. Build the project first or specify custom path.".to_string())
+}
+
+#[tauri::command]
+pub async fn get_engine_status(
+    state: tauri::State<'_, RunnerState>,
+) -> Result<EngineState, String> {
+    let mut guard = state.child.lock().await;
+    if let Some(child) = guard.as_mut() {
+        match child.try_wait() {
+            Ok(Some(_status)) => {
+                *guard = None;
+                Ok(EngineState {
+                    is_running: false,
+                    pid: None,
+                })
+            }
+            Ok(None) => Ok(EngineState {
+                is_running: true,
+                pid: child.id(),
+            }),
+            Err(_) => {
+                *guard = None;
+                Ok(EngineState {
+                    is_running: false,
+                    pid: None,
+                })
+            }
+        }
+    } else {
+        Ok(EngineState {
+            is_running: false,
+            pid: None,
+        })
+    }
+}
+
+#[tauri::command]
+pub async fn stop_engine(
+    app: AppHandle,
+    state: tauri::State<'_, RunnerState>,
+) -> Result<(), String> {
+    let mut guard = state.child.lock().await;
+    if let Some(mut child) = guard.take() {
+        let _ = child.kill().await;
+        let _ = app.emit("engine-status", EngineState {
+            is_running: false,
+            pid: None,
+        });
+        let now = SystemTime::now().duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH).unwrap().as_millis() as u64;
+        let _ = app.emit("engine-log", EngineLogEntry {
+            channel: "info".to_string(),
+            text: "--- Engine process terminated by user ---".to_string(),
+            timestamp: now,
+        });
+    }
+    Ok(())
+}
+
+#[tauri::command]
+pub async fn launch_engine(
+    app: AppHandle,
+    state: tauri::State<'_, RunnerState>,
+    config: LauncherConfig,
+) -> Result<u32, String> {
+    let mut guard = state.child.lock().await;
+    if guard.is_some() {
+        return Err("Engine is already running".to_string());
+    }
+
+    let bin_path = find_engine_binary(&config.engine_binary_path)?;
+
+    #[cfg(unix)]
+    {
+        use std::os::unix::fs::PermissionsExt;
+        if let Ok(meta) = fs::metadata(&bin_path) {
+            let mut perms = meta.permissions();
+            perms.set_mode(0o755);
+            let _ = fs::set_permissions(&bin_path, perms);
+        }
+    }
+
+    let mut cmd = Command::new(&bin_path);
+    if let Some(parent) = bin_path.parent() {
+        if parent.as_os_str().len() > 0 {
+            cmd.current_dir(parent);
+        }
+    }
+
+    // Set Environment Variables
+    if !config.romfs_path.is_empty() {
+        cmd.env("TOTK_ROMFS", &config.romfs_path);
+    }
+    cmd.env("TOTK_HUD", if config.graphics.enable_hud { "1" } else { "0" });
+    cmd.env("TOTK_HEADLESS", if config.graphics.headless { "1" } else { "0" });
+    cmd.env("TOTK_DOCK", if config.display.docked_mode { "1" } else { "0" });
+    cmd.env("TOTK_VSYNC", &config.display.vsync_mode);
+    cmd.env("TOTK_ASPECT", &config.display.aspect_ratio);
+    cmd.env("TOTK_ASYNC_REPLAY", if config.graphics.async_replay { "1" } else { "0" });
+    cmd.env("TOTK_VK_DEVICE_INDEX", config.graphics.vulkan_device_index.to_string());
+
+    let res_scale = match config.display.resolution.as_str() {
+        "720p" => "0.666",
+        "1080p" => "1.0",
+        "1440p" => "1.333",
+        "4K" => "2.0",
+        _ => "1.0",
+    };
+    cmd.env("TOTK_RES_SCALE", res_scale);
+
+    if !config.controller.active_guid.is_empty() {
+        cmd.env("TOTK_PAD_GUID", &config.controller.active_guid);
+    }
+    cmd.env("TOTK_PAD_SWAP_AB_XY", if config.controller.swap_ab_xy { "1" } else { "0" });
+    cmd.env("TOTK_PAD_DEADZONE_L", config.controller.left_deadzone.to_string());
+    cmd.env("TOTK_PAD_DEADZONE_R", config.controller.right_deadzone.to_string());
+    cmd.env("TOTK_PAD_TRIGGER_THRESH", config.controller.trigger_threshold.to_string());
+    cmd.env("TOTK_PAD_GYRO", if config.controller.gyro_enabled { "1" } else { "0" });
+    cmd.env("TOTK_PAD_GYRO_SENS", config.controller.gyro_sensitivity.to_string());
+    cmd.env("TOTK_PAD_RUMBLE", if config.controller.rumble_enabled { "1" } else { "0" });
+    cmd.env("TOTK_PAD_RUMBLE_INTENSITY", config.controller.rumble_intensity.to_string());
+    cmd.env("TOTK_PAD_HD_RUMBLE", if config.controller.hd_rumble_lra_sim { "1" } else { "0" });
+
+    // Patches
+    for patch in &config.patches {
+        if patch.enabled {
+            match patch.id.as_str() {
+                "patch_zero_heap" => { cmd.env("TOTK_PATCH_ZERO_HEAP_FALLBACK", "1"); },
+                "patch_loose_romfs" => { cmd.env("TOTK_PATCH_LOOSE_ROMFS", "1"); },
+                "patch_60fps_lock" => { cmd.env("TOTK_PATCH_60FPS", "1"); },
+                "patch_disable_drs" => { cmd.env("TOTK_PATCH_DISABLE_DRS", "1"); },
+                "patch_ultrawide" => { cmd.env("TOTK_PATCH_ULTRAWIDE", "1"); },
+                _ => {},
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    for (k, v) in &config.custom_env {
+        cmd.env(k, v);
+    }
+
+    cmd.stdout(std::process::Stdio::piped());
+    cmd.stderr(std::process::Stdio::piped());
+
+    let mut child = cmd.spawn().map_err(|e| format!("Failed to spawn {}: {}", bin_path.display(), e))?;
+    let pid = child.id().unwrap_or(0);
+
+    let stdout = child.stdout.take();
+    let stderr = child.stderr.take();
+
+    let app_clone1 = app.clone();
+    if let Some(out) = stdout {
+        tokio::spawn(async move {
+            let mut reader = BufReader::new(out).lines();
+            while let Ok(Some(line)) = reader.next_line().await {
+                let now = SystemTime::now().duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH).unwrap().as_millis() as u64;
+                let channel = if line.contains("[totk-gfx]") || line.contains("[vulkan]") || line.contains("NVN") {
+                    "gfx"
+                } else if line.contains("[totk-thread]") || line.contains("svc") || line.contains("thread") {
+                    "thread"
+                } else if line.contains("SIGSEGV") || line.contains("fault") || line.contains("error") || line.contains("fatal") || line.contains("***") {
+                    "error"
+                } else {
+                    "info"
+                };
+                let _ = app_clone1.emit("engine-log", EngineLogEntry {
+                    channel: channel.to_string(),
+                    text: line,
+                    timestamp: now,
+                });
+            }
+        });
+    }
+
+    let app_clone2 = app.clone();
+    if let Some(err) = stderr {
+        tokio::spawn(async move {
+            let mut reader = BufReader::new(err).lines();
+            while let Ok(Some(line)) = reader.next_line().await {
+                let now = SystemTime::now().duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH).unwrap().as_millis() as u64;
+                let channel = if line.contains("[totk-gfx]") {
+                    "gfx"
+                } else if line.contains("SIGSEGV") || line.contains("fault") || line.contains("error") || line.contains("fatal") || line.contains("***") {
+                    "error"
+                } else {
+                    "error"
+                };
+                let _ = app_clone2.emit("engine-log", EngineLogEntry {
+                    channel: channel.to_string(),
+                    text: line,
+                    timestamp: now,
+                });
+            }
+        });
+    }
+
+    let _ = app.emit("engine-status", EngineState {
+        is_running: true,
+        pid: Some(pid),
+    });
+
+    *guard = Some(child);
+    Ok(pid)
+}

+// Helper: Format Bytes
+function formatBytes(bytes: number): string {
+  if (bytes === 0) return "0 Bytes";
+  const k = 1024;
+  const sizes = ["Bytes", "KiB", "MiB", "GiB", "TiB"];
+  const i = Math.floor(Math.log(bytes) / Math.log(k));
+  return parseFloat((bytes / Math.pow(k, i)).toFixed(2)) + " " + sizes[i];
+}
+
+// 1. Tab Switching
+function initTabs() {
+  const tabs = navTabs.querySelectorAll<HTMLAnchorElement>(".tab");
+  tabs.forEach((tab) => {
+    tab.addEventListener("click", () => {
+      tabs.forEach((t) => t.classList.remove("tab-active"));
+      tab.classList.add("tab-active");
+
+      const targetId = tab.getAttribute("data-tab");
+      document.querySelectorAll(".tab-content-panel").forEach((panel) => {
+        panel.classList.add("hidden");
+      });
+      if (targetId) {
+        document.getElementById(targetId)?.classList.remove("hidden");
+      }
+    });
+  });
+}
+
+// 2. Theme Switching
+function applyTheme(themeName: string) {
+  const finalTheme = themeName === "sky-island-light" ? "sky-island-light" : "sky-island-dark";
+  docRoot.setAttribute("data-theme", finalTheme);
+  themeToggle.checked = finalTheme === "sky-island-dark";
+  if (currentConfig) {
+    currentConfig.theme = finalTheme;
+    invoke("save_config", { config: currentConfig }).catch(console.error);
+  }
+}
+
+themeToggle.addEventListener("change", () => {
+  const nextTheme = themeToggle.checked ? "sky-island-dark" : "sky-island-light";
+  applyTheme(nextTheme);
+});
+
+// 3. Config Hydration
+async function loadConfig() {
+  try {
+    currentConfig = await invoke<LauncherConfig>("get_config");
+    applyTheme(currentConfig.theme === "sky-island-light" ? "sky-island-light" : "sky-island-dark");
+
+    // Hydrate Dashboard & Quick settings
+    dashDockedToggle.checked = currentConfig.display.docked_mode;
+    dashHudToggle.checked = currentConfig.graphics.enable_hud;
+    dashHeadlessToggle.checked = currentConfig.graphics.headless;
+    dashResolutionSelect.value = currentConfig.display.resolution;
+    dashVsyncSelect.value = currentConfig.display.vsync_mode;
+
+    // Hydrate Graphics
+    gfxAspectSelect.value = currentConfig.display.aspect_ratio;
+    gfxDeviceIdx.value = String(currentConfig.graphics.vulkan_device_index);
+    gfxAsyncReplayToggle.checked = currentConfig.graphics.async_replay;
+    gfxFullscreenToggle.checked = currentConfig.display.fullscreen;
+
+    // Hydrate Controller Settings
+    sliderDeadzoneL.value = String(Math.round(currentConfig.controller.left_deadzone * 100));
+    labelDeadzoneL.innerText = `${sliderDeadzoneL.value}%`;
+    stickLDeadzoneVisual.style.width = `${sliderDeadzoneL.value}%`;
+    stickLDeadzoneVisual.style.height = `${sliderDeadzoneL.value}%`;
+
+    sliderDeadzoneR.value = String(Math.round(currentConfig.controller.right_deadzone * 100));
+    labelDeadzoneR.innerText = `${sliderDeadzoneR.value}%`;
+    stickRDeadzoneVisual.style.width = `${sliderDeadzoneR.value}%`;
+    stickRDeadzoneVisual.style.height = `${sliderDeadzoneR.value}%`;
+
+    sliderTriggerThresh.value = String(Math.round(currentConfig.controller.trigger_threshold * 100));
+    labelTriggerThresh.innerText = `${sliderTriggerThresh.value}%`;
+
+    ctrlProfileSelect.value = currentConfig.controller.profile;
+    ctrlRumbleToggle.checked = currentConfig.controller.rumble_enabled;
+    sliderRumbleIntensity.value = String(Math.round(currentConfig.controller.rumble_intensity * 100));
+    labelRumbleIntensity.innerText = `${sliderRumbleIntensity.value}%`;
+    ctrlHdRumbleToggle.checked = currentConfig.controller.hd_rumble_lra_sim;
+
+    ctrlGyroToggle.checked = currentConfig.controller.gyro_enabled;
+    sliderGyroSens.value = String(Math.round(currentConfig.controller.gyro_sensitivity * 10));
+    labelGyroSens.innerText = `${(Number(sliderGyroSens.value) / 10).toFixed(1)}x`;
+
+    // Hydrate Assets
+    assetsRomfsInput.value = currentConfig.romfs_path || "";
+    if (currentConfig.romfs_path) {
+      await checkRomfs(currentConfig.romfs_path);
+    } else {
+      updateRomfsUI({
+        status: "Missing",
+        total_files: 0,
+        total_bytes: 0,
+        path: "",
+        has_version: false,
+        has_pack: false,
+        has_shader: false,
+      });
+    }
+
+    renderPatchesTable();
+    updateTargetProfileStat();
+  } catch (err) {
+    console.error("Failed to load config:", err);
+  }
+}
+
+function updateTargetProfileStat() {
+  if (!currentConfig) return;
+  const dock = currentConfig.display.docked_mode ? "Docked" : "Handheld";
+  statTargetProfile.innerText = `${currentConfig.display.resolution} ${dock}`;
+}
+
+// 4. Patches Table
+function renderPatchesTable() {
+  if (!currentConfig || !patchesTableBody) return;
+  patchesTableBody.innerHTML = "";
+
+  const patchHooks: Record<string, string> = {
+    patch_zero_heap: "f_80006a920 + 0x8008ac138",
+    patch_loose_romfs: "nn::fs::Rom",
+    patch_60fps_lock: "sead::Framework::calc",
+    patch_disable_drs: "agl::pdr::DRC",
+    patch_ultrawide: "sead::Camera",
+  };
+
+  currentConfig.patches.forEach((patch, idx) => {
+    const tr = document.createElement("tr");
+    tr.className = "hover border-b border-base-300";
+
+    const tdCheck = document.createElement("td");
+    tdCheck.className = "text-center";
+    const chk = document.createElement("input");
+    chk.type = "checkbox";
+    chk.className = "checkbox checkbox-primary checkbox-sm";
+    chk.checked = patch.enabled;
+    chk.addEventListener("change", () => {
+      currentConfig.patches[idx].enabled = chk.checked;
+      saveCurrentConfig();
+    });
+    tdCheck.appendChild(chk);
+
+    const tdName = document.createElement("td");
+    const hookSymbol = patchHooks[patch.id] || "src/runtime/";
+    tdName.innerHTML = `
+      <div class="flex flex-col gap-0.5">
+        <span class="font-semibold text-xs text-[#EDE6D1]">${escapeHtml(patch.name)}</span>
+        <span class="badge badge-ghost font-mono text-[10px] text-[#63A7BC] border border-base-300/80 w-fit">${escapeHtml(hookSymbol)}</span>
+      </div>
+    `;
+
+    const tdDesc = document.createElement("td");
+    tdDesc.className = "text-xs text-base-content/75";
+    tdDesc.innerText = patch.description;
+
+    tr.appendChild(tdCheck);
+    tr.appendChild(tdName);
+    tr.appendChild(tdDesc);
+    patchesTableBody.appendChild(tr);
+  });
+}
+
+async function saveCurrentConfig() {
+  if (!currentConfig) return;
+  try {
+    await invoke("save_config", { config: currentConfig });
+  } catch (err) {
+    console.error("Failed to save config:", err);
+  }
+}
+
+// Save Patches button
+btnSavePatches?.addEventListener("click", async () => {
+  await saveCurrentConfig();
+  const originalText = btnSavePatches.innerText;
+  btnSavePatches.innerText = "Saved ✓";
+  setTimeout(() => {
+    btnSavePatches.innerText = originalText;
+  }, 1500);
+});
+
+// 5. Controller Enumeration & Live Calibration
+async function refreshControllers() {
+  try {
+    const controllers = await invoke<GamepadInfo[]>("list_controllers");
+    ctrlDeviceSelect.innerHTML = "";
+
+    if (controllers.length === 0) {
+      const opt = document.createElement("option");
+      opt.value = "";
+      opt.innerText = "No gamepads detected (Plug in USB or Bluetooth)";
+      ctrlDeviceSelect.appendChild(opt);
+      statActiveController.innerText = "None Detected";
+      stopPollingStick();
+      return;
+    }
+
+    controllers.forEach((pad) => {
+      const opt = document.createElement("option");
+      opt.value = pad.id;
+      const ffTag = pad.has_rumble ? "[Haptics ✓]" : "[No FF]";
+      opt.innerText = `${pad.name} • ${pad.power_info} ${ffTag}`;
+      ctrlDeviceSelect.appendChild(opt);
+    });
+
+    currentControllerId = controllers[0].id;
+    ctrlDeviceSelect.value = currentControllerId;
+    statActiveController.innerText = controllers[0].name.split("(")[0].trim();
+    startPollingStick(currentControllerId);
+  } catch (err) {
+    console.error("Failed to list controllers:", err);
+  }
+}
+
+btnRefreshControllers?.addEventListener("click", refreshControllers);
+ctrlDeviceSelect?.addEventListener("change", () => {
+  currentControllerId = ctrlDeviceSelect.value;
+  if (currentControllerId) {
+    startPollingStick(currentControllerId);
+    const selectedOpt = ctrlDeviceSelect.options[ctrlDeviceSelect.selectedIndex];
+    statActiveController.innerText = selectedOpt.text.split("•")[0].trim();
+  } else {
+    stopPollingStick();
+  }
+});
+
+function startPollingStick(id: string) {
+  stopPollingStick();
+  controllerPollTimer = window.setInterval(async () => {
+    try {
+      const stick = await invoke<StickPreview>("poll_controller_preview", { id });
+      // Map stick coordinates to circular bounds (radius 60px max)
+      const maxR = 52;
+      const lxPx = stick.lx * maxR;
+      const lyPx = -stick.ly * maxR;
+      const rxPx = stick.rx * maxR;
+      const ryPx = -stick.ry * maxR;
+
+      stickLDot.style.transform = `translate(${lxPx}px, ${lyPx}px)`;
+      stickRDot.style.transform = `translate(${rxPx}px, ${ryPx}px)`;
+      stickLCoords.innerText = `X: ${stick.lx.toFixed(2)} | Y: ${stick.ly.toFixed(2)} | ZL: ${stick.l2.toFixed(2)}`;
+      stickRCoords.innerText = `X: ${stick.rx.toFixed(2)} | Y: ${stick.ry.toFixed(2)} | ZR: ${stick.r2.toFixed(2)}`;
+    } catch (_) {}
+  }, 40);
+}
+
+function stopPollingStick() {
+  if (controllerPollTimer !== null) {
+    clearInterval(controllerPollTimer);
+    controllerPollTimer = null;
+  }
+}
+
+// Sliders Events
+sliderDeadzoneL?.addEventListener("input", () => {
+  const val = Number(sliderDeadzoneL.value);
+  labelDeadzoneL.innerText = `${val}%`;
+  stickLDeadzoneVisual.style.width = `${val * 2}%`;
+  stickLDeadzoneVisual.style.height = `${val * 2}%`;

+btnTestRumble?.addEventListener("click", async () => {
+  if (!currentControllerId) return;
+  const intensity = (currentConfig?.controller.rumble_intensity || 1.0);
+  try {
+    await invoke("test_rumble", {
+      id: currentControllerId,
+      lowFreq: intensity,

+      durationMs: 250,
+    });

+    console.error("Test rumble error:", err);
+  }

+// 6. Assets & RomFS
+async function checkRomfs(path: string) {
+  try {
+    const status = await invoke<RomFsStatus>("validate_romfs", { path });
+    updateRomfsUI(status);
+  } catch (err) {
+    console.error("Validate romfs error:", err);
+  }
+}
+
+function updateRomfsUI(status: RomFsStatus) {
+  if (status.status === "PackedVerified") {
+    statRomfsStatus.innerText = "Packed ✓";
+    statRomfsStatus.className = "badge badge-sm badge-success text-[11px] font-mono";
+    statRomfsDetail.innerText = `romfs.bin (${formatBytes(status.total_bytes)})`;
+
+    setAlertStatus(checkVersion, true, "Ready in romfs.bin");
+    setAlertStatus(checkPack, true, "Ready in romfs.bin");
+    setAlertStatus(checkShader, true, "Ready in romfs.bin");
+  } else if (status.status === "LooseVerified") {
+    statRomfsStatus.innerText = "Loose Ready";
+    statRomfsStatus.className = "badge badge-sm badge-primary text-[11px] font-mono";
+    statRomfsDetail.innerText = `${status.total_files} files (${formatBytes(status.total_bytes)})`;
+
+    setAlertStatus(checkVersion, status.has_version, status.has_version ? "Found" : "Missing");
+    setAlertStatus(checkPack, status.has_pack, status.has_pack ? "Found" : "Missing");
+    setAlertStatus(checkShader, status.has_shader, status.has_shader ? "Found" : "Missing");
+  } else {
+    statRomfsStatus.innerText = "Missing";
+    statRomfsStatus.className = "badge badge-sm badge-error text-[11px] font-mono";
+    statRomfsDetail.innerText = "Path not set";
+
+    setAlertStatus(checkVersion, false, "Missing");
+    setAlertStatus(checkPack, false, "Missing");
+    setAlertStatus(checkShader, false, "Missing");
+  }
+}
+
+function setAlertStatus(el: HTMLElement, ok: boolean, label: string) {
+  if (!el) return;
+  el.className = `alert ${ok ? "border-[#2E8B57]/50 bg-[#12582C]/20" : "border-[#C93B3B]/40 bg-[#C93B3B]/10"} text-xs flex items-center justify-between p-3 rounded-sm border`;
+  const icon = el.querySelector(".indicator-icon") as HTMLElement;
+  const statusSpan = el.querySelector(".status-text") as HTMLElement;
+  if (icon) icon.innerText = ok ? "✓" : "⚠️";
+  if (statusSpan) {
+    statusSpan.className = `badge badge-sm ${ok ? "badge-success text-white" : "badge-error text-white"} font-mono text-[10px]`;
+    statusSpan.innerText = label;
+  }
+}
+
+btnBrowseRomfs?.addEventListener("click", async () => {
+  try {
+    const selected = await open({
+      directory: true,
+      multiple: false,
+      title: "Select Tears of the Kingdom RomFS Folder",
+    });
+    if (selected && typeof selected === "string") {
+      assetsRomfsInput.value = selected;
+      if (currentConfig) {
+        currentConfig.romfs_path = selected;
+        await saveCurrentConfig();
+      }
+      await checkRomfs(selected);
+    }
+  } catch (err) {
+    console.error("Browse path error:", err);
+  }
+});
+
+assetsRomfsInput?.addEventListener("change", async () => {
+  const p = assetsRomfsInput.value.trim();
+  if (currentConfig) {
+    currentConfig.romfs_path = p;
+    await saveCurrentConfig();
+  }
+  await checkRomfs(p);
+});
+
+btnPackRomfs?.addEventListener("click", async () => {
+  const source = assetsRomfsInput.value.trim();
+  if (!source) {
+    alert("Please select a valid RomFS source path first.");
+    return;
+  }
+  packProgressContainer.classList.remove("hidden");
+  packProgressBar.value = 5;
+  packProgressStatus.innerText = "Initializing RomFS compression...";
+  packProgressPct.innerText = "5%";
+
+  try {
+    await invoke("pack_romfs", {
+      engineDir: ".",
+      sourcePath: source,
+      outputBin: "data/romfs/romfs.bin",
+    });
+  } catch (err: any) {
+    packProgressStatus.innerText = `Pack error: ${err}`;
+  }
+});
+
+listen("romfs-pack-progress", (event: any) => {
+  const data = event.payload;
+  packProgressBar.value = Math.round(data.percent);
+  packProgressPct.innerText = `${Math.round(data.percent)}%`;
+  packProgressStatus.innerText = data.message;
+  if (data.is_finished) {
+    setTimeout(() => {
+      if (data.success && currentConfig) {
+        currentConfig.romfs_path = "data/romfs/romfs.bin";
+        assetsRomfsInput.value = currentConfig.romfs_path;
+        checkRomfs(currentConfig.romfs_path);
+      }
+    }, 1000);
+  }
+});
+
+// 7. Graphics & Quick Controls
+dashDockedToggle?.addEventListener("change", () => {
+  if (currentConfig) {
+    currentConfig.display.docked_mode = dashDockedToggle.checked;
+    updateTargetProfileStat();
+    saveCurrentConfig();
+  }
+});
+
+dashHudToggle?.addEventListener("change", () => {
+  if (currentConfig) {
+    currentConfig.graphics.enable_hud = dashHudToggle.checked;
+    saveCurrentConfig();
+  }
+});
+
+dashHeadlessToggle?.addEventListener("change", () => {
+  if (currentConfig) {
+    currentConfig.graphics.headless = dashHeadlessToggle.checked;
+    saveCurrentConfig();
+  }
+});

+  if (currentConfig) {
+    currentConfig.display.resolution = dashResolutionSelect.value;
+    updateTargetProfileStat();

+});

+  }
+});

+    currentConfig.graphics.async_replay = gfxAsyncReplayToggle.checked;
+    saveCurrentConfig();
+  }
+});

+gfxFullscreenToggle?.addEventListener("change", () => {

+// 8. Engine Execution & Status
+async function checkEngineStatus() {
+  try {
+    const st = await invoke<EngineState>("get_engine_status");
+    updateEngineStateUI(st);
+  } catch (err) {
+    console.error("Check status error:", err);
+  }
+}
+
+function updateEngineStateUI(st: EngineState) {
+  if (st.is_running) {
+    statusBadge.className = "badge badge-success gap-2 font-mono text-xs py-2.5 px-3 animate-pulse border border-[#2E8B57]";
+    statusBadge.innerHTML = `<span class="inline-block w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-white"></span><span>Running PID ${st.pid}</span>`;
+    btnLaunch.classList.add("hidden");
+    btnStop.classList.remove("hidden");
+    quickPidLabel.innerText = `PID: ${st.pid}`;
+  } else {
+    statusBadge.className = "badge badge-ghost gap-2 font-mono text-xs py-2.5 px-3 border border-base-300";
+    statusBadge.innerHTML = `<span class="inline-block w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-base-content/40"></span><span>Engine Idle</span>`;
+    btnLaunch.classList.remove("hidden");
+    btnStop.classList.add("hidden");
+    quickPidLabel.innerText = "PID: --";
+  }
+}
+
+btnLaunch?.addEventListener("click", async () => {
+  if (!currentConfig) return;
+  try {
+    const pid = await invoke<number>("launch_engine", { config: currentConfig });
+    updateEngineStateUI({ is_running: true, pid });
+    appendLog({
+      channel: "info",
+      text: `[Launcher] Spawned ultratears engine with PID ${pid}`,
+      timestamp: Date.now(),
+    });
+  } catch (err: any) {
+    alert(`Launch failed: ${err}`);
+    appendLog({
+      channel: "error",
+      text: `[Launcher Error] ${err}`,
+      timestamp: Date.now(),
+    });
+  }
+});
+
+btnStop?.addEventListener("click", async () => {
+  try {
+    await invoke("stop_engine");
+    updateEngineStateUI({ is_running: false, pid: null });
+  } catch (err) {
+    console.error("Stop engine error:", err);
+  }
+});
+
+listen("engine-status", (event: any) => {
+  updateEngineStateUI(event.payload);
+});
+
+// 9. Console & Log Streaming
+listen("engine-log", (event: any) => {
+  const entry: EngineLogEntry = event.payload;
+  logHistory.push(entry);
+  if (logHistory.length > 2000) {
+    logHistory.shift();
+  }
+  appendLog(entry);
+});
+
+function appendLog(entry: EngineLogEntry) {
+  const filter = logFilterChannel.value;
+  const search = logSearchInput.value.toLowerCase().trim();
+
+  const matchFilter = filter === "all" || entry.channel === filter;
+  const matchSearch = !search || entry.text.toLowerCase().includes(search);
+
+  logLineCounter.innerText = `${logHistory.length} lines`;
+
+  if (!matchFilter || !matchSearch) return;
+
+  const pre = document.createElement("pre");
+  let colorClass = "text-[#DCD09F]/80";
+  if (entry.channel === "gfx" || entry.text.includes("[totk-gfx]") || entry.text.includes("[NVN]")) {
+    colorClass = "text-[#63A7BC] font-semibold";
+  } else if (entry.channel === "thread" || entry.text.includes("[totk-thread]")) {
+    colorClass = "text-[#E8BF40] font-semibold";
+  } else if (entry.channel === "error" || entry.text.includes("signal") || entry.text.includes("SIGSEGV") || entry.text.includes("fault") || entry.text.includes("error") || entry.text.includes("Error")) {
+    colorClass = "text-[#C93B3B] font-bold";
+  } else if (entry.channel === "info" || entry.text.includes("[totk]")) {
+    colorClass = "text-[#DCD09F]";
+  }
+
+  pre.className = colorClass;
+  pre.innerHTML = `<code>${escapeHtml(entry.text)}</code>`;
+  logOutputContainer.appendChild(pre);
+
+  if (logAutoscrollToggle.checked) {
+    consoleTerminal.scrollTop = consoleTerminal.scrollHeight;
+  }
+}
+
+function escapeHtml(text: string): string {
+  return text
+    .replace(/&/g, "&amp;")
+    .replace(/</g, "&lt;")
+    .replace(/>/g, "&gt;");
+}
+
+function refilterLogs() {
+  logOutputContainer.innerHTML = "";
+  const filter = logFilterChannel.value;
+  const search = logSearchInput.value.toLowerCase().trim();
+
+  logHistory.forEach((entry) => {
+    const matchFilter = filter === "all" || entry.channel === filter;
+    const matchSearch = !search || entry.text.toLowerCase().includes(search);
+    if (matchFilter && matchSearch) {
+      appendLog(entry);
+    }
+  });
+}
+
+logFilterChannel?.addEventListener("change", refilterLogs);
+logSearchInput?.addEventListener("input", refilterLogs);
+
+btnClearLogs?.addEventListener("click", () => {
+  logHistory = [];
+  logOutputContainer.innerHTML = "";
+  logLineCounter.innerText = "0 lines";
+});
+
+btnExportLogs?.addEventListener("click", async () => {
+  const text = logHistory.map((l) => `[${new Date(l.timestamp).toISOString()}] [${l.channel}] ${l.text}`).join("\n");
+  try {
+    const filePath = await save({
+      defaultPath: "ultratears-session.log",
+      filters: [{ name: "Log Files", extensions: ["log", "txt"] }],
+    });
+    if (filePath) {
+      const blob = new Blob([text], { type: "text/plain" });
+      const a = document.createElement("a");
+      a.href = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
+      a.download = filePath;
+      a.click();
+    }
+  } catch (err) {
+    console.error("Export log error:", err);
+  }
+});
+
+// Initialization
+window.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", async () => {
+  initTabs();
+  await loadConfig();
+  await refreshControllers();
+  await checkEngineStatus();
+});

diff --git a/tools/launcher/vite.config.ts b/tools/launcher/vite.config.ts
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..9dbbe35

+++ b/tools/launcher/vite.config.ts

+import { defineConfig } from "vite";
+// @ts-expect-error type error without @types/node package
+import process from "node:process";
+const host = process.env.TAURI_DEV_HOST;
+
+// https://vite.dev/config/
+export default defineConfig(() => ({
+
+  // Vite options tailored for Tauri development and only applied in `tauri dev` or `tauri build`
+  //
+  // 1. prevent Vite from obscuring rust errors
+  clearScreen: false,
+  // 2. tauri expects a fixed port, fail if that port is not available
+  server: {
+    port: 1420,
+    strictPort: true,
+    host: host || false,
+    hmr: host
+      ? {
+          protocol: "ws",
+          host,
+          port: 1421,
+        }
+      : undefined,
+    watch: {
+      // 3. tell Vite to ignore watching `src-tauri`
+      ignored: ["**/src-tauri/**"],
+    },
+  },
+}));
